If these Ad Tools Terms conflict with the Advertising Terms, the Ad Tools Terms will control with respect to the specific Ad Tool. By enabling, accessing, or using an Ad Tool, or by providing data or materials through it, you agree to the terms applicable to that Ad Tool and represent that you have authority to bind the applicable entity.1. Conversion Tools.Your access to and use of the Conversion Tools is subject to the Conversion Terms, which are incorporated into these Ad Tools Terms by reference.2. Audience Data2.1. Audience Tools. OpenAI may make Audience Tools available for you to provide Audience Data to OpenAI. Subject to the Advertising Terms and these Ad Tools Terms, you may access and use the Audience Tools during the term solely as permitted under these Ad Tools Terms. You will not: (a) use the Audience Tools unlawfully, in violation of OpenAI Policies, or in a manner that infringes third-party rights; (b) Reverse Engineer, interfere with, disrupt, extract data from, or circumvent protective measures for the Audience Tools; or (c) buy, sell, transfer, or share any keys, credentials, or other access mechanisms for the Audience Tools except as permitted by OpenAI. 2.2. Audience Data. Audience Data will be deemed information and materials associated with a Campaign under the Advertising Terms. You will provide Audience Data to OpenAI for the Access Purpose, including for OpenAI to create custom audiences for you. You represent and warrant that: (a) Audience Data is first-party data and is not purchased, licensed, received, or otherwise obtained from data brokers, data marketplaces, or other third-party data suppliers; provided that you may use a service provider, including an identity-resolution provider, to process, format, hash, transmit, match, or resolve Audience Data on your behalf, so long as the provider is not the source of Audience Data and does not append, enrich, or supplement Audience Data with third-party data; (b) you have provided all required notices and obtained and will maintain all rights, consents, permissions, and legal bases required to collect, use, share, and provide Audience Data to OpenAI for the purposes described in these Ad Tools Terms; (c) your use of the Audience Tools and provision of Audience Data will comply with applicable law and will not cause OpenAI to violate applicable law; (d) you will not provide Prohibited Data, harmful code, or data that infringes, violates, or misappropriates any third-party rights; and (e) you will promptly notify OpenAI of any actual or threatened complaint or Claim relating to Audience Data and reasonably cooperate with OpenAI in responding. 2.3. No Guarantee. OpenAI does not guarantee audience availability, match rates, audience size, reach, delivery, performance, accuracy, or that any person belongs to or does not belong to a particular audience. OpenAI may reject, remove, limit, suppress, or require modification of any Audience Data or audience as permitted under the Advertising Terms or these Ad Tools Terms.2.4. Audience Restrictions. You will not use Audience Tools, Audience Data, related Reporting Data, or other data collected in connection with the Audience Tools and Advertising Services to (a) identify or re-identify any OpenAI user, (b) infer sensitive or other information about any person, or (c) create, adjust, or modify audience segments, profiles, or data sets that explicitly or implicitly identify, target, or relate to OpenAI, its users, or its brands, except through campaign functionality expressly made available by OpenAI.2.5. Processing of Personal Data. Except for Restricted Processing, you and OpenAI each act as an independent Data Controller with respect to any Personal Data Processed through the Audience Tools. For Restricted Processing only, you are the Data Controller and OpenAI is the Data Processor. The Ad Tools DPA is incorporated by reference and applies to Processing of Personal Data.3. AI Creative Tools 3.1. Generated Creatives. OpenAI may make available AI-powered Creative Tools that use Ad Materials to generate, modify, transform, optimize, localize, translate, select, assemble, or otherwise adapt advertising creatives for a Campaign, including by dynamically tailoring them to the context of a user’s interaction with OpenAI. Creative Tools may make Generated Creatives available for your review or automatically apply Generated Creatives to your Campaign in accordance with your Campaign settings. You can manage or disable Creative Tools through applicable Ads Manager controls. Any Generated Creative that you approve or use or that is applied to your Campaign will be deemed an Ad under the Advertising Terms. OpenAI may access, retrieve, and use Ad Materials as needed to provide the Creative Tools and Advertising Services. 3.2. Advertiser Materials and Responsibilities. You are responsible for: (a) all Ad Materials you provide or make available and all Campaign settings in your Account; (b) all Generated Creatives that you approve or use, or that are applied to your Campaign; (c) the accuracy, completeness, currency, and substantiation of all material representations in Ad Materials and Generated Creatives, including, but not limited to, claims related to pricing, availability, Advertiser Products, testimonials, endorsements, affiliations, sponsorships, performance, qualifications, risks, and expected results; and (d) any disclosure about AI-generated or synthetic content required by applicable law or OpenAI Policies. You represent and warrant that you have all rights, licenses, permissions, and consents needed for OpenAI to access, retrieve, and use the Ad Materials as described in these Ad Tools Terms and for Generated Creatives to be used in connection with the Advertising Services. Your indemnification obligations under the Advertising Terms apply to Claims related to any Generated Creative that you approve or use, or that is applied to your Campaign.3.3. No Guarantees. Restrictions. You will not: (a) provide any Prohibited Data through the Creative Tools;  (b) use the Creative Tools to generate Ads that violate applicable law or OpenAI Policies; (c) create or use an unauthorized digital replica, voice, likeness, endorsement, or representation of another person; (d) impersonate a person or organization or falsely suggest an affiliation, sponsorship, endorsement, or approval; (e) create or distribute fraudulent, deceptive, or misleading synthetic media; (f) bypass protective measures or other restrictions on the Creative Tools; or (g) configure or use the Creative Tools to reveal, reproduce, infer, or imply access to non-public information about an OpenAI user or the user’s interactions with OpenAI.
